  - New `ir/ScopePlanner.java` (pure Java, testable): `plan(Source, entryTypes, maxClasses)` reads the text of every non-library class once through a `Source` callback (text is dropped after each class; only small int arrays stay), finds per class: source (Intent getter, SharedPreferences getter, superclass name ending in `Provider;`), sink (any API of the `TaintIr` sink table, via new package-private `TaintIr.sinkOwners()` / `TaintIr.isSink()`), and the app classes it invokes. Selected in priority order until `maxClasses` (6000): A source AND sink in one class, B source or sink, C entry components, D callees of A+B (helpers), E callers of A+B (way in), F callers of E. Returns ascending indexes + stats (`total`, `marked`, `entries`, `capped`, `cancelled`).
  - `MainActivity.ssaFindings`: unchanged for apps with <= 6000 classes. Above that it calls the planner, parses only the selected classes and sets `ssaScopeNote` ("reduced scope N/M classes", plus ", capped" when the cap cut candidates). The path bar shows e.g. `[SSA taint: +2 flow(s) (reduced scope 410/9200 classes)]`. A flow with reach NO in a reduced scope gets the detail note "analysed in a reduced scope, so a missing path ... can be an artefact". The old dedupe (class + title) is unchanged.
  - Known limits (kept honest): a flow whose chain goes more than 2 hops away from a marked class is not seen; an entry path through an unselected class shows as "not reachable"; the planner reads every class text once (time grows with app size, cancel is checked per class); the `Taint on SSA` export still parses every non-library class (no reduced scope there, it can run out of memory on a huge app).
  - Tests `IrScopeTest` (7): marked classes only, entry always kept, callee depth 1 + caller depth 1 and 2 (3 hops stays out), Provider subclass is a source, cap prefers source+sink classes and reports `capped`, cancel returns an empty plan, and an end-to-end case (300 filler classes + entry + helper with the flow: 2 classes selected, flow found, reach YES).
  - Next: owner rebuilds (no change expected on the 6 lab apps: all are below 6000 classes), then runs Deep scan on a BIG real app (the 60 MB Google Play app of the 0.45.0 stress test or any app with > 6000 classes) and sends the screenshot of the path bar (`reduced scope ...`) + the time it took + any new taint cards. Then decide: raise the hop limit, or make the export use the same reduced scope.

## Version 0.56.0 (previous)
- 0.56.0 (unverified on device, NOT compiled with Gradle/Kotlin; Java part compiled with javac 21, 76/76 IR tests pass locally: 62 old + 13 `IrBridgeTest` + 1 new in `IrTaintTest`). Version 0.56.0 / code 128. **Roadmap P1 step 2: the SSA taint now adds findings to the Deep scan** (rule id `taint-flow`, so REACH_RULES / AI triage / SARIF treat them like every taint-flow finding). **0.55.2 results (owner)**: build OK, 5 reports. Expected changes happened: AndroGoat and InsecureBankv2 only-old 0; InjuredAndroid FlagTwelve now `both` (marked not reachable), RCEActivity only-new; Damn Vulnerable Bank 1/0/0; no failures, max 1.9 s.
  - **Finding while reading the 5 ONLY NEW lines**: `SecurityScanner.scanSmali` merges findings with the same title inside ONE class into one card kept at the first method (`merged[f.title]`, count++). So the old list can only answer "does this class have a finding with this title", never "this method". AndroGoat `ContentProviderActivity.query` / `.update` and InsecureBankv2 `TrackUserContentProvider.update` were NOT misses of the old engine: they sit in the same class + title as the delete card. They are real flows, but already counted by the old card. `InsecureShop PrivateActivity.onCreate` (different class, not reachable) is still unexplained; with this version it is added as a finding (reach NO) so it is visible either way. `RCEActivity` is a true miss of the old engine (confirmed on the smali).
  - `ir/SsaBridge.java` (pure Java, testable): runs `CallGraph` + `TaintIr.analyze(g, false, ...)`, drops every flow whose class already has an old finding with the same title (class + title, NOT method + title), groups the rest per sink method + title, returns plain `Item`s (class, method, char offset of the sink instruction, title, CWE, severity, confidence, detail, fix, reachable, entry, path, count). Cap `MAX_ITEMS = 40`.
  - Confidence mirrors the Kotlin engine: real source 2 = HIGH, level 1 (provider parameter, unknown call) = MEDIUM, validation-like call on the chain (same regex as `TaintEngine.validationRe`, scanned over every method of the chain + the sink method) lowers one step, SharedPreferences source = LOW, a flow no entry reaches lowers one more step. Severity drops one level when the confidence ends LOW. `reach` is set from the new call graph (`YES` with `Entry:`/`Path:`, or `NO`), so `reachPass` does not trace it again; `applyReach` adds the usual note to `NO` Intent flows (detail starts with `Data from Intent.`).
  - `MainActivity.runDeepScan`: after `xlinker.link()` (before `aggregate`) calls the new `ssaFindings(defs, appPkg, found)`; old list = taint findings of `found` as `Old(classType + "->", title)`. Apps with more than 6000 non-library classes skip it (the IR keeps every parsed class); the path bar shows `[SSA taint: +N flow(s)]`, `[SSA taint: no extra flow]` or `[SSA taint skipped: ...]`. Any exception is caught and counted under rule id `taint-ssa`.
  - `TaintIr.compare` (the export) now has a third bucket: a flow with the same class + title as an old finding is `MERGED` (not `ONLY NEW`); header text says the scanner uses the result. Test `flowInAClassWhoseOldCardWasMergedIsNotCountedAsNew`.
  - `Capabilities.kt`: one sentence about the SSA engine for the AI assistant.
  - `tools/benchmark/taint_bench.py` + `expected_taint/*.json` (tools only, no app code): scores the SSA taint export per flow with evidence levels. First run on the 0.55.2 reports: 19 flows = 2 TP-verified, 8 TP-documented, 1 LIBRARY (`net.gotev` UploadService, not filtered by `scopeOf`: both engines report it), 8 UNREVIEWED (5 are AndroGoat `ContentProviderActivity` SQL, plus InjuredAndroid `TestBroadcastReceiver$b`, DVB `CurrencyRates`, InsecureShop `PrivateActivity`). Recall is `n/a` on purpose: every expected entry so far was written after seeing the tool's output, so 9/9 would be circular. Do NOT quote those numbers as accuracy.
  - Next: owner rebuilds, runs Deep scan on InjuredAndroid, InsecureShop, AndroGoat, InsecureBankv2, OVAA and Damn Vulnerable Bank and sends the reports (or just the Security cards + the path bar text). Expected: InjuredAndroid gains the `RCEActivity` card (CRITICAL, reach YES); InsecureShop gains `PrivateActivity` (reach NO) unless the old card for that class existed; AndroGoat / InsecureBankv2 / DVB / OVAA: no new card. Anything else new is reviewed by hand (true flow or false positive). Then re-export "Taint on SSA": ONLY NEW should be 0 everywhere.

## Version 0.55.2 (previous)
- 0.55.2 (unverified on device, NOT compiled with Gradle/Kotlin; Java part compiled with javac 21, 62/62 IR tests pass locally). Version 0.55.2 / code 127. **Smali read (owner)**: `FlagTwelveProtectedActivity.onCreate` really has `getIntent().getStringExtra("totally_secure")` -> `WebView.loadData` (branch where the scheme is not https): the OLD finding is a TRUE positive. The exact shape (copied into a scratch test) IS found by `TaintIr` when the class is an entry or when all methods are analysed, and is NOT found when the class is not an entry and `reachableOnly = true`. So the cause is scope, not logic: 0.55.0/0.55.1 analysed only reachable methods, the Kotlin engine reports flows in every class. Fix: the export now calls `TaintIr.analyze(g, false, old)` (every app method); every flow still says `entry:` path or "not reachable in the call graph", and the report header states the scope. Test `flowInAClassNoEntryReachesIsStillReportedAndMarkedNotReachable`. Unverified guess: FlagTwelve is not in the entry set (not exported in the manifest, or only reachable through a path the graph lacks); the owner can confirm in the Manifest tab.
  - `RCEActivity.onCreate` (only-new, CRITICAL) is a TRUE positive: `getIntent().getData()` -> `Uri.getQueryParameter("binary")` / `("param")` -> StringBuilder (`filesDir/files/` + binary + " " + param) -> `Runtime.exec(String)`, taken when the `combined` query parameter is null. The Kotlin engine missed it (try/catch region around the whole block and `p1` reused for the Uri, the Process and the TextView); the SSA engine separates those versions. First clear win of P1 over the old engine; the old engine's miss is not investigated (not needed if P1 replaces it).
  - Still expected after rebuild: AndroGoat / InsecureBankv2 lose their only-old line (provider parameters, 0.55.1), InjuredAndroid loses FlagTwelve only-old and keeps RCEActivity only-new.

## Version 0.55.1 (previous)
- (0.55.1 notes follow)
- 0.55.1 (unverified on device, NOT compiled with Gradle/Kotlin; Java part compiled with javac 21, 61/61 IR tests pass locally). Version 0.55.1 / code 126. **0.55.0 taint reports (owner)**: OVAA both 2 / only old 0 / only new 0 (incl. one cross-method flow via `InsecureLoggerService.getDumpFile`); InsecureShop both 3 / 0 / 0 (5 flows, 3 distinct sink+title keys); InsecureBankv2 both 1 / only old 1 / new 0 (the one flow goes through the field `ViewStatement.uname`); InjuredAndroid both 2 / only old 1 / only new 1; AndroGoat both 0 / only old 1 / new 0. No failures anywhere, 59-600 ms per app.
  - **Only-old cause 1 (fixed here)**: InsecureBankv2 `TrackUserContentProvider.delete` and AndroGoat `ContentProviderActivity.delete` (SQL). The Kotlin engine seeds the object parameters of ContentProvider methods (query / insert / update / delete / openFile / openAssetFile / call / bulkInsert) as a real source (level 1); the new engine had no entry-parameter sources. `TaintIr` now does the same: instance method with one of those names on a class whose super chain ends in a framework `...Provider` -> every object / array parameter is a real source "a ContentProvider method parameter". Tests: `contentProviderParametersAreSources`, `sameMethodOnANonProviderClassIsNotASource`.

## Version 0.55.0 (previous)
- (0.55.0 notes follow)
- 0.55.0 (unverified on device, NOT compiled with Gradle/Kotlin; Java part compiled with javac 21, 59/59 IR tests pass locally incl. 19 new). Version 0.55.0 / code 125. **Roadmap P1, first experimental step: taint on SSA over the CallGraph, a SEPARATE report, no scanner behaviour changed** (no finding, severity or dedupe reads it). **0.54.5 results (owner)**: AndroGoat `isRooted1` has only its definition + Kotlin metadata, no `invoke` anywhere = dead code, the old class-level trace over-approximated: accepted, nothing to fix. InjuredAndroid 3/3 exact, NEEDS A LOOK = 0 (dropping the `...Activity` base requirement fixed it; the original cause is still unknown). Compare status: OVAA 12/12, InsecureBankv2 5/5, InsecureShop 6/6, Damn Vulnerable Bank 1/1, InjuredAndroid 3/3, AndroGoat 6/7 + 1 accepted (dead code). The graph grew on InjuredAndroid (entries 26 -> 50, reached methods 817 -> 983), OLD NO / NEW YES = 0: if the taint shows extra reach, look at the loosened onClick rule first.
  - New `ir/TaintIr.java` (pure Java): per-method taint on SSA values (one taint value per SSA value, monotone, phis join, iterate to a fixpoint, flow-insensitive per value). Taint value = bitmask of symbolic parameter slots (receiver = slot 0, wide = one slot) + at most one REAL source (Intent extra level 2, SharedPreferences level 1). Builders / containers / arrays are mutated in place and the taint is also added to every alias (move, check-cast, phi), so `v5 = v4; v5.append(x); v4.toString()` works. Fields: field-based (per field reference), real sources only, readers are re-queued when a field grows. Calls: app callees come from the CallGraph edges of that call site (only invoke edges: static / direct / super / virtual / interface; callback, clinit, icc and trigger edges pass no arguments and are ignored); framework callees use the same source / sanitizer / builder / pure / sink tables as the Kotlin TaintEngine (same titles, so the comparison is exact). Summary per method: parameters that reach the return value, a real source that is returned, parameters that reach a sink (chain of methods, depth <= 6, <= 16 refs per slot). Solved with a worklist over the callers (each method at most 40 analyses). Only REACHABLE methods are analysed by default (`analyze(g, true, old)`); `false` analyses every app method.
  - Report (`TaintIr.Result.format()`): counts, flows with a real source (source, sink API + method + insn, chain of methods, entry path from the CallGraph or "not reachable"), and a COMPARE block against the Kotlin `taint-flow` / `taint-xclass` findings (key = sink method + title): both / only old / only new, each listed. UI: Export report -> "Taint on SSA, experimental (.txt, needs a Deep scan first)" (`MainActivity.exportReport`, format `taint`, file `<pkg>-taint.txt`), same plumbing as "Call graph compare". The Kotlin part is NOT compiled here: it copies the call pattern of the cgcompare branch (`classDefs`, `agentIndexedText`, `SecurityScanner.methodKeyAt`, `entryTypeMap()`), so a compile error there would be a typo, not a design problem.
  - Tests: `IrTaintTest.java` (19): straight flow, constant, sanitizer, StringBuilder, alias through move, phi, source into a callee parameter, source returned by a callee, 3-level chain, unreachable skipped, parameter alone is not a flow, field between methods, interface call through RTA, wide parameter alignment (both directions), recursion, SharedPreferences level, compare text, and a 40-seed fuzz (random call graphs with recursion, fields, builders, diamonds): 0 failures, deterministic report.
  - Known limits (kept honest): no path sensitivity; taint is per SSA value, not per program point; a callee never mutates its parameters for the caller (only return value and sinks are tracked); unknown library calls do not propagate (same as the Kotlin engine); fields are not per object; lambdas / reflection follow whatever the CallGraph gives.
  - Next: owner rebuilds, runs "Taint on SSA" on OVAA, InsecureBankv2, InsecureShop, AndroGoat, InjuredAndroid and sends the .txt files. Read the COMPARE block first: ONLY OLD = something the new engine misses (fix before anything else), ONLY NEW = either a real cross-method flow the old engine could not see or a false positive (check each chain by hand). Only after the numbers look right do we let the findings use it (P1 step 2), and not before.

## Version 0.53.0 (previous)
- 0.53.0 (unverified on device, NOT compiled with Gradle/Kotlin; the Java IR files WERE compiled with javac 21 and their tests run with a minimal JUnit stub). Version 0.53.0 / code 117. **Roadmap P0: IR layer, no scanner behaviour changed** (nothing in the Kotlin code calls it yet).

  - `CallGraph.build(classes, componentTypes, Mode.RTA|CHA, extraEntries)`: exact static/direct/super edges, virtual/interface by RTA (CHA available), CHA fallback for unlinked sites (edge kind ends with `~`), component lifecycle entry points (`ENTRY_NAMES`), `<clinit>` edges, Thread.start->run and AsyncTask triggers, callback edges (object created in the same method and passed to a framework method with a known callback parameter type). `pathTo(key)`, `callersOf`, `calleesOf`, `toDot`, `stats`. Not modelled: reflection, native, ICC launches (the Kotlin call graph in SecurityScanner still does those), callbacks registered with an object made in another method.

  - Suspects if Gradle fails: none expected (plain Java 17, no Android API). If a test fails, decide first whether the test or the code is wrong.
  - Next: (1) run the IR over a REAL app (parse every class of the indexed APK, build the CFG + SSA of every method, count exceptions and time; this is the stress test the hand-written smali cannot give); (2) feed `CallGraph` from `runDeepScan` next to the Kotlin `CallIndex` and compare the reach answers on OVAA / InsecureBankv2 before replacing anything; (3) benchmark harness numbers as the baseline; then P1 (taint on SSA).

Result: `reach = "YES~"` + chain "Approximate path (class level): ...". UI/report text says "approximate / probably reachable". `FindingPolish.applyReach` is unchanged (only touches `NO`). `Trace.approximate` flag added.
  - `CallIndex.callersOf` is now a lazy hash lookup (rebuilt when edges were added); the old linear scan would be too slow with the extra edges.
  - **InsecureBankv2 gap 1** (`CryptoHardcodedKeyRule`, id unchanged `crypto-hardcoded-key`): also reports a String FIELD named *key / *secret / *passphrase that is a `.field ... = "lit"` constant or is set from a `const-string` by `iput-object`/`sput-object`, in a class that uses javax.crypto and has no "AndroidKeyStore". HIGH if the class builds a SecretKeySpec and reads the field, else MEDIUM; max 2 per class.
  - **InsecureBankv2 gap 2** (`LogCredentialObjectRule`): also fires when the method builds a log message with a StringBuilder and reads a String field whose name contains passw / pwd / token / secret / credential (username alone is not enough). MEDIUM.
  - Tests: 6 new approx tests in `CallGraphTest.kt`, new `RuleGapTest.kt` (6 tests). Regexes were dry-run in Python only. `tools/benchmark/bench.py` counts "Probably reachable" as reach. The two `known_gap` flags in `expected/com.android.insecurebankv2.json` are left in place until the device confirms them (then remove the flags).
  - Suspects if it fails to compile: `traceApprox` (HashSet/HashMap use), the local `fun report(...)` in `CryptoHardcodedKeyRule`, the long regex strings in it, `callersOf` generics.
  - Checks on device: (1) build; (2) OVAA: 19/19 and no new YES~ on findings that were YES; (3) InsecureBankv2: both former gaps FOUND, target 11/11 (AI-FP verdicts are unstable: judge by rule hit, not by verdict); (4) Wikipedia: SingleWebViewActivity / CommunicationBridge become YES~ or stay NO: read the path; (5) report time on NewPipe / Fossify not noticeably longer.

## Version 0.50.0 (previous)
- 0.50.0 (unverified, NOT compiled; no kotlinc in the sandbox). Version 0.50.0 / code 109. Call graph follows implicit Intents with a CUSTOM action.
  - `SecurityScanner.collectEdges`: a method that has a custom action string constant (`const-string`, looks like `pkg.action.NAME`, not `android.*`, max 8) AND calls `Intent.<init>(String...)` or `Intent.setAction` gets a synthetic edge `actionKey(action)` -> that method (same idea as the 0.49.4 launch edges).
  - `SecurityScanner.componentActions(xml, pkg)`: component type -> custom actions of its intent-filters (all components, not only exported; alias -> target). `MainActivity.actionsOfClass` (cached in `actionMap`, reset with `entryMap`) feeds `trace(..., actionsOf = ...)`, which adds the callers of `actionKey(a)` next to the launch callers when a node's component is first seen.
  - Deliberately NOT followed: framework actions (VIEW, SEND, ...: every browser/link builder would connect to every filter), ComponentName built from strings, PendingIntent. Possible false YES: any method with that string constant that also builds some Intent from a String.
  - Checks on device: (1) build; (2) OVAA: WebViewActivity still YES, and the chain now can go MainActivity$3 -> (action oversecured.ovaa.action.WEBVIEW) -> WebViewActivity; (3) Wikipedia SingleWebViewActivity: YES or NO, read the chain/Note; (4) NewPipe/Fossify report time unchanged; no new YES on rules outside REACH_RULES.

## Version 0.49.0-0.49.4 (previous; see the patch files)

### 0.49.0
- 0.49.0 (unverified, NOT compiled; no kotlinc in the sandbox). **Feature version.** Phase 3 of the engine plan: cross-class taint. Version 0.49.0 / code 103.
  - New file `TaintLinker.kt` (XHit / XEdge / XMethod summaries, `XRecorder`, `TaintLinker.add()` thread-safe, `TaintLinker.link()` stage B). `TaintEngine.summarize()` (ScanRulesTaint.kt) = stage A: the register analysis runs again per class with SYMBOLIC taints: object params are labelled `@P<slot>`, the result of a call into app code `@R<callee key>` (same class too); real sources keep their normal label. Per method it records: sink hits with a symbolic label, call edges (tainted register -> callee key + slot), and labels reaching return-object. `State.raise` ignores symbolic labels (they never leave their method). `TaintEngine.scan` (in-class rule `taint-flow`) is unchanged.
  - Stage B: direction 1 = real source -> callee param slot -> (callee chain, depth 4) -> sink: finding at the SINK method; direction 2 = sink receives `@R<callee>` and the callee (transitively) returns a real source. Rule id `taint-xclass`, same titles/CWEs as taint-flow, confidence MEDIUM at best (LOW when >3 methods, a validation-like call in the sink method, or an inherent-MEDIUM sink), SharedPreferences sources skipped. `SecurityScanner.dedupe`: `taint-flow` absorbs `taint-xclass` of the same class and title (new `Absorb.sameTitle`).
  - Wiring (MainActivity.runDeepScan): `xlinker = TaintLinker(appPkg)`; each worker calls `TaintEngine.summarize(...)` next to `collectEdges` (non-LIB classes only); after join `found.addAll(xlinker.link())` unless cancelled. Errors counted as rule id `taint-xclass`. Caps: 64 edges/method, 16 hits/method, 150k edges total, 60 findings.
  - Limits (also in Capabilities.SCANNER): owner class as written in the invoke (virtual / interface dispatch to subclasses, lambdas, reflection, fields of other classes, collections between calls not followed); first-hop real sources only for edges (a source passed through an app helper's return value becomes `@R...`).
  - Suspects if it fails to compile: `TaintLinker.kt` (`for ((lab, lv) in m.rets)`, `sortedWith(compareBy(...))`, `listOf(xm)` as `List<XMethod>`), `ScanRulesTaint.kt` (`recordEdges`, `summarize`, `rec.hit(...)` argument order), `SecurityScanner.dedupe` (rewritten loop).
  - Checks on device: (1) build; (2) AndroGoat Smart scan still 29/30 and no crash/slow-down (stage A doubles the taint work on app classes; compare scan time with 0.48.4); (3) OVAA: no new false HIGH, finding count about the same (taint-xclass should mostly be absorbed or absent); (4) any app with a helper class that opens an Intent value (e.g. a Utils.loadUrl(String) -> WebView.loadUrl) shows a `taint-xclass` finding at the helper with "crosses 1 method call(s)"; (5) OVAA 6 (PICK) still needs the MainActivity.onActivityResult smali.
  - Next (phase 4): declarative rule spec (Source/Sink/Sanitizer/Condition as data) so a rule is a table row; phase 5 optional Python/Androguard side tool.

  - **arsc entry names, `ArscPool.readStringEntries`:** now honours the type chunk flags at +9: FLAG_SPARSE (0x01, pairs idx+offset/4), FLAG_OFFSET16 (0x02, u16 offset/4, 0xFFFF = none), plain u32 (-1 = none). Compact entries (entry flags 0x0008) are skipped. Out-of-range offsets / short entries are skipped (`continue`) instead of aborting the whole type. Before, a sparse type could pair names with wrong values silently.

  - **Firebase DB rule (30/30):** new code rule `FirebaseDbUrlRule` (id `firebase-db-url`, ScanRules.kt, registered after TaintFlowRule): exact host regex `https://[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\.(firebaseio\.com|firebasedatabase\.app)` over every const-string of the class, independent of the Firebase SDK and of AndroGoatChecks' literal window; skips LIB; MEDIUM / confidence HIGH; placeholder project ids filtered. CWE-284 (already in VulnKnowledge).
  - **resources.arsc entry names (ScanRulesResources.kt):** `ArscPool` is now a random-access `StrPool` reader; new `namedStrings()` walks RES_TABLE_PACKAGE chunks (typeStrings/keyStrings pools at +268/+276 of the package header) and the first RES_TABLE_TYPE chunk per type id whose type name is "string", mapping entry name -> global-pool value (Res_value dataType 0x03; complex entries skipped). `ResourceScanner.scanArsc` order: namedStrings -> pool candidates -> byte scan (full fallbacks). `ResourceContext` gained `namesAvailable`; `ResSecretNamesRule` (retitled "Credential-like value in string resources") now also runs on the arsc names (api_key / stripe_secret / auth_token ... on the BUILT apk). Stats line shows "(with entry names)". Verified against a synthetic arsc fixture (global pool + package + string type) in Python: names map to values.
  - **Noise reduction:** INFO hidden by default (`secMinRank` default 3; filter cycle 3 -> 2 -> 1 -> 4(+INFO) -> 3; chips reset to 3). `SecurityScanner.aggregate(list)` merges same rule+class repeats and collapses cross-class INFO into one xN card; applied after the deep scan and to the resource findings. `FindingPolish` lowers non-HIGH-confidence findings in OBF classes one level (like LIB); `secIsVisible` hides OBF findings with the Libs filter EXCEPT CRITICAL/HIGH (a real addJavascriptInterface in obfuscated app code stays visible). hiddenLibs counter now covers LIB + OBF.
  - **Taint (ScanRulesTaint.kt):** SharedPreferences.getString/getStringSet/getAll are level-1 sources ("SharedPreferences.x() ... rooted devices / restored backups"); `hasSource` gate widened accordingly. Level-2 proven chains still auto-raise confidence HIGH; validation-like calls lower it.
  - **Attack surface (ReportBuilder.kt):** `attackSurface()` / `attackSurfaceHtml()` - table of exported components (alias resolved) with their intent-filter actions + sorted permission list; wired into `buildReport()` (Markdown, before the security section) and `ReportBuilder.html()` (new optional `manifestXml` param).
  - Capabilities.SCANNER: NOISE REDUCTION paragraph. Version 0.46.0 / code 90.
  - Suspects if it fails to compile: `ScanRulesResources.kt` (`ArscPool.StrPool` inner class, `namedStrings`/`readPackage`/`readStringEntries` bounds math), `ScanRules.kt` FirebaseDbUrlRule (`ctx.text.indexOf(first)` offset, string escapes), `ReportBuilder.kt` (local `compRe` shadows nothing; Regex imports are unneeded - kotlin.text.Regex is default).
  - Checks on device: (1) AndroGoat: benchmark 30/30 (Firebase line OK). (2) Real 60 MB APK: default list shows LOW+ only; INFO reachable via the "I" chip / "Filter: ALL (+INFO)"; entropy/root/emulator noise merged into xN cards; obfuscated SDK noise hidden unless CRITICAL/HIGH. (3) An APK with a string resource named like `api_key`/`auth_token` and a random-looking value: "Credential-like value in string resources" MEDIUM from resources.arsc. (4) Report (md + html) starts with an "Attack surface" table. (5) Scan time/memory unchanged: arsc named parse is one pass, capped at MAX_NAMED=40000 entries.

## Older notes
Versions 0.12.1 - 0.45.0 moved unchanged to `docs/HANDOFF_ARCHIVE.md` (0.48.3 cleanup). Read it only when a past decision matters.
